OUTPUT: set turn="review", fill the "review" object, and set every other section to null.
REVIEW TURN. Independently audit EVERY candidate in `candidates`; each is a
hypothesis from the discovery turn, not a trusted conclusion. Return exactly one
decision per candidate_id.

Return obligation_class (execution_compatibility per D4, target_coverage per D5),
scope_basis, trigger_basis and the five checks of J1, each with status
established/refuted/unknown, a specific reason and exact evidence:
scope_contract (J1a, D2); feasible_trigger (J1b): for execution compatibility a
finite actual target execution, for target coverage a valid supported
behavior/setup whose route the target blocks; observable_consequence (J1c);
alternatives_ruled_out (J1d, D8), including counterevidence supplied with the
candidate; identity_preserved (J1e; for timing also T1).

Use effect blocked_target_coverage only for target_coverage. For target_coverage
fill every coverage_witness field; for the other class use empty strings. These
fields do not replace the citations of each check.

decision=keep requires all five checks established with source and harness
evidence, a finite_witness and no decisive missing fact. decision=dismiss requires
a grounded refutation or equivalent route (J6). Otherwise return uncertain and
request the missing facts (E3). For maintenance, unresolved timing does not
invalidate a currently established obligation (J2).

Then audit the whole scope again (J4): assess every selected change ID and the
four foundations with evidence, challenge both dismissals (J6) and retained
gaps (J1). Out-of-scope items (D7) are not obligations. New evidence contains
contracts retrieved for earlier requests (`evidence`); `citation_errors` lists earlier
citations the program could not verify; cite valid locations instead. Request
exact symbols or paths only for decisive missing facts (E3).
If the evidence establishes a distinct obligation that is NOT among `candidates`
(for example one that only became visible with the new evidence), report it in
`new_candidates` with the same fields as a discovery blocker and mark its change
group as blocked in the scope audit. It is reviewed in a separate turn; do not
add a decision for it here.
